# Tidemark: numbered 3x3 production sheets

Generated by `tools/tidemark/checklist.py` from the card checklist (do not edit). One cell is one card illustration; cells run left to right, top to bottom. Read `ART_BIBLE.md` first. Order of work: anniversary sheets, then alternate art, then the historical library.

Summary: 77 sheets (anniversary 18, alternate 2, historical 57), 657 cells used, 36 cells deliberately left empty (listed under each sheet; never fill them with invented cards).

## TM-S001: Crownlands: people (anniversary)

File: `tidemark-production/sheets/TM-S005.png`

1. **Choir Novice** (TMC-0597, Abyssal Choir, Unit) `TMI-0597`  
   Subject: A young novice in a grey robe learns to sing underwater in a cave pool.  
   Direction: Abyssal Choir: the deep ocean, trenches, sunken ruins and lonely night shores. Palette ink black, deep blue-violet, bone grey, with sparing cold bioluminescence (the one faction where glow is normal). Drowned cultists in sodden robes; creatures are deep-sea animals made vast and strange, unsettling rather than gory. One clear subject (or a small readable group) in a believable setting; full figure or three-quarter view in the middle of the frame, with room above the head and at both sides for cropping. Ordinary register: natural light, muted colour, worn and practical details; no halo or magical light; the deep sea's natural cold bioluminescence is allowed, sparingly. Light: storm light.

   Direction: Free Fleets: working ships, busy quays, open sea, coves and harbour taverns. Palette weathered wood, canvas, sea blue, faded reds. Sailors in practical working clothes; traders and privateers, not cartoon pirates; ships drawn with believable rigging. The ship in three-quarter view on the water with the waterline and full rig visible; sea and sky set the weather and the scale. Legendary register: the most dramatic composition this game allows, but restrained: one strong light source, no excessive ornament, no glowing halos. Light: bright windy daylight. Anniversary alternate art: a new illustration of the same card, a different moment and composition from its original art, in the same painterly style; ordinary card size and window, no special frame.
5. **Black Tide Leviathan** (TMC-0273, Abyssal Choir, Unit) `TMI-A08`  
   Subject: The Black Tide Leviathan seen from beneath, a vast dark shape crossing the moonlit surface above a tiny diver.  
   Direction: Abyssal Choir: the deep ocean, trenches, sunken ruins and lonely night shores. Palette ink black, deep blue-violet, bone grey, with sparing cold bioluminescence (the one faction where glow is normal). Drowned cultists in sodden robes; creatures are deep-sea animals made vast and strange, unsettling rather than gory. The creature as the clear subject in its habitat with a readable silhouette and the whole body visible; for big creatures include a small scale cue (a boat, a person, a tree). Legendary register: the most dramatic composition this game allows, but restrained: one strong light source, no excessive ornament, no glowing halos. Anniversary alternate art: a new illustration of the same card, a different moment and composition from its original art, in the same painterly style; ordinary card size and window, no special frame.

   Subject: A colossal grey leviathan surfaces beside a tiny fishing boat, barnacles on its ancient hide.
